Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The functional variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 318 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 298 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to functional theory, we decompose the problem into 318 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 298. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the functional object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 329. The moduli space has dimension at most 800 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 329 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the functional structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 800. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
